//! Immutable source releases with a database-authoritative activation pointer.
use super::*;
use crate::store_owner::Owner;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
#[path = "staging.rs"]
mod staging;
struct Stage(PathBuf);
impl Drop for Stage {fn drop(&mut self){let _=std::fs::remove_dir_all(&self.0);}}

fn directory(options: &Options) -> Result<PathBuf, String> {
    options
        .positionals
        .first()
        .map(PathBuf::from)
        .ok_or_else(|| "a deployment directory is required".into())
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#5.1 — deployment aliases share ownership, control and worker paths.
fn canonical_directory(options: &Options) -> Result<PathBuf, String> {
    let requested = directory(options)?;
    if !requested.exists() {
        snapback4_core::private_fs::directory(&requested).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    }
    let dir = requested.canonicalize().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    snapback4_core::private_fs::directory(&dir).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    Ok(dir)
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.000.000#8 — validate before any writable ownership or command lock.
pub(crate) fn existing_deployment(requested: &Path) -> Result<PathBuf, String> {
    let missing = || format!("no current release in {}; run `snapback4 deploy {}` first", requested.display(), requested.display());
    if !requested.join("store.sqlite").is_file() { return Err(missing()); }
    let dir = requested.canonicalize().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    inspect_current(&dir)?;
    Ok(dir)
}

// SQLite READONLY can create WAL sidecars. A stopped store's existing owner
// lock makes immutable inspection safe; a live host supplies its own status.
fn inspect_current(dir: &Path) -> Result<u64, String> {
    use rusqlite::{Connection, OpenFlags, OptionalExtension};
    let database = dir.join("store.sqlite").canonicalize().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let missing = || format!("no current release in {}; run `snapback4 deploy {}` first", dir.display(), dir.display());
    let owner = match snapback4_core::private_fs::auxiliary_file(&database,
        &snapback4_core::private_fs::owner_path(&database), false, 0) {
        Ok(owner) => owner,
        Err(error) if error.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => {
            // Relocated/legacy deployments may not yet have a sidecar beside
            // the resolved DB. Establish a complete current source first; only
            // a known deployment may let SQLite create its ordinary WAL files.
            return pointer_current(dir, &database);
        },
        Err(error) => return Err(format!("{}; existing ownership sidecar unavailable: {error}", missing())),
    };
    match owner.try_lock() {
        Ok(()) => {},
        Err(std::fs::TryLockError::WouldBlock) => {
            match crate::release_control::request(dir, crate::release_control::Command::Status {after:0, items_after:None, event:None}) {
                Ok(status) => return status["release"].as_u64().filter(|n|*n>0).ok_or_else(missing),
                Err(error) => {
                    // A killed host's watchdogs retain ownership during their
                    // bounded cleanup. Preserve serve's existing restart wait.
                    if !crate::store_owner::stopped_host(&database) {
                        // A second source directory may alias the same DB. Its
                        // source cannot control this host, but its token still
                        // belongs to the resolved database. Establish current
                        // source before permitting ordinary read-only WAL work.
                        return pointer_current(dir, &database).map_err(|_| format!("{}; {error}",missing()));
                    }
                    let deadline=std::time::Instant::now()+std::time::Duration::from_secs(5);
                    loop {
                        match owner.try_lock() {
                            Ok(()) => break,
                            Err(std::fs::TryLockError::WouldBlock) if std::time::Instant::now()<deadline => std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(25)),
                            _ => return Err(format!("{}; {error}",missing())),
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        },
        Err(error) => return Err(error.to_string()),
    }
    if !snapback4_core::private_fs::owner_is_named(&database, &owner) {
        return Err("store ownership changed during release inspection; retry".into());
    }
    #[cfg(unix)]
    let bytes = { use std::os::unix::ffi::OsStrExt; database.as_os_str().as_bytes() };
    #[cfg(not(unix))]
    let bytes = database.to_str().ok_or("invalid store path")?.as_bytes();
    let mut uri = String::from("file:");
    for byte in bytes {
        if byte.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || b"/._-".contains(byte) { uri.push(*byte as char); }
        else { uri.push_str(&format!("%{byte:02X}")); }
    }
    let mut wal = database.as_os_str().to_os_string(); wal.push("-wal"); let wal = PathBuf::from(wal);
    let mut shm = database.as_os_str().to_os_string(); shm.push("-shm"); let shm = PathBuf::from(shm);
    let has_wal = match std::fs::metadata(&wal) {
        Ok(meta) => meta.len() > 0,
        Err(e) if e.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => false,
        Err(e) => return Err(e.to_string()),
    };
    if has_wal && !shm.is_file() { return Err("store has a WAL without its shared-memory sidecar; run deploy on this stopped deployment to recover it before inspection".into()); }
    // Immutable SQLite omits WAL reads: use it only for the checkpointed main
    // image. Existing WAL+SHM can be read normally under the same exclusion.
    uri.push_str(if has_wal { "?mode=ro" } else { "?mode=ro&immutable=1" });
    let conn = Connection::open_with_flags(uri, OpenFlags::SQLITE_OPEN_READ_ONLY | OpenFlags::SQLITE_OPEN_URI).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    let release: Option<String> = conn.query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key='release'", [], |row|row.get(0)).optional().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    let release = match release {
        Some(value) => value,
        None => std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("current")).map_err(|_| missing())?,
    };
    release.trim().parse::<u64>().ok().filter(|n|*n>0).ok_or_else(missing)
}

fn pointer_current(dir: &Path, database: &Path) -> Result<u64, String> {
    let missing = || format!("no current release in {}; deploy first", dir.display());
    let pointer = std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("current")).map_err(|_| missing())?;
    let pointer = pointer.trim().parse::<u64>().ok().filter(|n|*n>0).ok_or_else(missing)?;
    bound_at(dir.join("releases").join(pointer.to_string()))?;
    let store = snapback4_core::Store::open_read_only(database).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    match store.meta("release").map_err(|e|e.to_string())? {
        Some(value) => value.parse::<u64>().ok().filter(|n|*n>0).ok_or_else(missing),
        None => Ok(pointer),
    }
}

/// `dev` and `serve` hold the store-owner lock for their lifetime. Release
/// commands route to the admitted server when it owns this deployment.
pub(crate) fn deployment_lock(dir: &Path) -> Result<Owner, String> {
    crate::store_owner::command(&dir.join("store.sqlite"))
}
fn current(dir: &Path, owner: &Owner) -> Result<Option<u64>, String> {
    let database = dir.join("store.sqlite");
    if database.exists() {
        let store = owner
            .open_store(std::time::Duration::from_secs(5))
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        if let Some(release) = store.meta("release").map_err(|e| e.to_string())? {
            return release
                .parse()
                .map(Some)
                .map_err(|_| "invalid release identity in store".into());
        }
    }
    // Compatibility with deployments made before the database owned this
    // pointer. The next activation records it transactionally.
    match std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("current")) {
        Ok(text) => text
            .trim()
            .parse()
            .map(Some)
            .map_err(|_| "invalid current release file".into()),
        Err(e) if e.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => Ok(None),
        Err(e) => Err(format!("cannot read current release: {e}")),
    }
}
pub(super) fn bound_at(root: PathBuf) -> Result<snapback4_lang::Bound, String> {
    let project = Project { root };
    if project.sources().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?.is_empty() {
        return Err("release has no Quarry source; select a complete immutable release".into());
    }
    let bound = project.compile().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    if !bound.diagnostics.is_empty() {
        return Err(bound
            .diagnostics
            .iter()
            .map(ToString::to_string)
            .collect::<Vec<_>>()
            .join("\n"));
    }
    Ok(bound)
}
fn check_change(
    dir: &Path,
    previous: Option<u64>,
    bound: &snapback4_lang::Bound,
    options: &Options,
    target_release: Option<u64>,
) -> Result<(), String> {
    if !options.flags.contains_key("check") && let Some(n) = previous {
        let old = bound_at(dir.join("releases").join(n.to_string()))?;
        let store = snapback4_core::Store::open_read_only(&dir.join("store.sqlite")).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let target_label = target_release.map(|n| format!("release {n}")).unwrap_or_else(|| "the new release".into());
        let lines = super::release_narration::compatibility(&store, &old.schema, &bound.schema, n, &target_label, target_release.is_none())?;
        for line in lines {
            eprintln!("{line}");
        }
    }
    if options.flags.contains_key("allow-destructive") {
        eprintln!(
            "deploy/rollback retain storage; --allow-destructive does not bypass validation or prune. To drop dormant storage: snapback4 prune {} --allow-destructive",
            dir.display()
        );
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn command_lock(dir: &Path) -> Result<std::fs::File, String> {
    #[cfg(unix)]
    {
        let file = snapback4_core::private_fs::auxiliary_file(
            &dir.join("store.sqlite"),
            &dir.join(".release-command"),
            true,
            0,
        )
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        file.lock().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        Ok(file)
    }
    #[cfg(not(unix))]
    Err("release commands require Unix".into())
}
fn target(dir: &Path) -> Result<Option<Owner>, String> {
    match deployment_lock(dir) {
        Ok(owner) => Ok(Some(owner)),
        Err(error) if error.contains("E_STORE_BUSY") => Ok(None),
        Err(error) => Err(error),
    }
}
fn status(
    dir: &Path,
    owner: Option<&Owner>,
    after: u64,
    items_after: Option<String>,
    event: Option<u64>,
) -> Result<serde_json::Value, String> {
    match owner {
        Some(owner) => owner
            .open_store(std::time::Duration::from_secs(5))
            .and_then(|mut s| s.release_status(after, items_after.as_deref(), event))
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string()),
        None => crate::release_control::request(
            dir,
            crate::release_control::Command::Status {
                after,
                items_after,
                event,
            },
        ),
    }
}
fn activate(
    dir: &Path,
    owner: Option<&Owner>,
    release: u64,
    previous: Option<u64>,
    revision: u64,
    bound: snapback4_lang::Bound,
    rollback: bool,
    restore_dormant: bool,
    allow_feed_adoption: bool,
    options: &Options,
    mut candidate: Option<&mut crate::release_files::Candidate>,
) -> Result<(), String> {
    let root = dir.join("releases").join(release.to_string());
    let digest = crate::release_files::digest(&root)?;
    if let Some(owner) = owner {
        let project = Project { root };
        let prepared = crate::native::prepare(&project, &bound).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        crate::dev_worker::prepare_release(&project, &bound.schema)?;
        crate::release_files::link_store(&project.root)?;
        crate::release_files::durable(&project.root)?;
        let mut pointer = crate::release_files::Pointer::prepare(dir, release, previous, rollback)?;
        let activation = snapback4_core::engine::release::ReleaseActivation {
            release,
            expected_previous: previous,
            expected_revision: Some(revision),
            restore_dormant,
            native_digest: prepared.installed.as_ref().map(|m| m.digest.clone()),
            operation: if rollback { "rollback" } else { "deploy" }.into(),
        };
        let store = owner
            .open_store(std::time::Duration::from_secs(5))
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let engine = snapback4_core::Engine::new_release_prepared(
            store,
            bound.schema,
            bound.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program),
            &activation,
            allow_feed_adoption,
        )
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        pointer.finish(engine.generation())?;
        receipt(release,engine.generation(),&engine.store().adoption_report,options);

    } else {
        let result = crate::release_control::request_observed(
            dir,
            crate::release_control::Command::Activate {
                release,
                expected_release: previous,
                expected_revision: revision,
                digest,
                rollback,
                restore_dormant,
                allow_feed_adoption,
            },
            |pending| { if let Some(candidate)=candidate.as_deref_mut() {candidate.pending=pending;} },
        )?;
        if options.json {println!("{}",result);} else {
            println!("release {release} is current as generation {}; {}; running serve adopted the release",result["generation"],result["adoption"].as_str().unwrap_or("adopted"));
            for fill in result["backfills"].as_array().into_iter().flatten().filter_map(|f|f.as_str()) {println!("  backfill: {fill}");}
            if options.flags.contains_key("verbose") {println!("{}",result["details"].as_str().unwrap_or(""));}
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}
fn report(verb: &str, result: Result<(), String>) -> i32 {
    // @ref LLP 3000#contended-read-serving — release completion owns no engine/lease; bound the final diagnostic drain.
    // The background writer must get a chance to emit adoption estimates before
    // this short-lived command exits. Broken/stalled output cannot hold exit.
    let _ = snapback4_core::diagnostics::drain(std::time::Duration::from_secs(3));
    match result {
        Ok(()) => 0,
        Err(error) => {
            eprintln!("{verb}: {error}");
            1
        }
    }
}

pub fn deploy(options: &Options) -> i32 {
    report(
        "deploy",
        (|| {
            let dir = canonical_directory(options)?;
            let _command = command_lock(&dir)?;
            let existed = dir.join("store.sqlite").exists();
            let checking=options.flags.contains_key("check");
            let _deployment = if checking {None} else {target(&dir)?};
            if let Some(owner) = &_deployment { owner.report_reclaim(options.flags.contains_key("verbose")); }
            let project = super::proof_source::project(options, "schema")?;
            let sources = super::proof_source::sources(&project, options, "schema")?;
            let bound = compiled_or_exit(super::proof_source::compile(&sources, options, "schema"), options.json);
            let mut saved_version = super::saved_version::SavedVersion::prepare(&project,&sources,options,&dir)?;
            if options.flags.contains_key("staging") {
                staging::classify(&dir, _deployment.as_ref(), existed, checking)?;
            }
            std::fs::create_dir_all(dir.join("releases")).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            let held = if checking {
                if dir.join("store.sqlite").exists() {snapback4_core::Store::open_read_only(&dir.join("store.sqlite")).and_then(|mut s|s.release_status(0,None,None)).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?}
                else {serde_json::json!({"release":null,"revision":0,"generation":1})}
            } else {status(&dir, _deployment.as_ref(), 0, None, None)?};
            let previous = if let Some(owner) = _deployment.as_ref() {
                current(&dir, owner)?
            } else {
                held["release"].as_u64()
            };
            let revision = held["revision"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0);
            check_change(&dir, previous, &bound, options, None)?;
            let releases = dir.join("releases").canonicalize().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            let stage = releases.join(format!(".stage-{}", std::process::id()));
            if stage.exists() {std::fs::remove_dir_all(&stage).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;}
            std::fs::create_dir(&stage).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            let _cleanup=Stage(stage.clone());
            std::fs::create_dir(stage.join("snapback")).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            let source_hash = {
                use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
                let mut hash = Sha256::new();
                let mut ordered = sources.iter().collect::<Vec<_>>();
                ordered.sort_by(|a,b| a.0.cmp(&b.0));
                for (name,text) in ordered {
                    hash.update((name.len() as u64).to_le_bytes()); hash.update(name.as_bytes());
                    hash.update((text.len() as u64).to_le_bytes()); hash.update(text.as_bytes());
                }
                format!("{:x}", hash.finalize())
            };
            for (name,text) in sources {
                let path=stage.join("snapback").join(name);
                if let Some(parent)=path.parent(){std::fs::create_dir_all(parent).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;}
                std::fs::write(path,text).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
            }
            crate::dev_worker::package(&project, &stage)?;
            // @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#2 — saved schemas prepare companion types in a private snapshot, never in working generated/.
            let private = if options.flags.contains_key("schema") || crate::dev_worker::source(&project).is_some() {
                Some(super::proof_source::Candidate::prepare(&project, &stage, &bound)?)
            } else { None };
            let build_project = private.as_ref().map(|candidate| &candidate.project).unwrap_or(&project);
            // A native job's module travels with the captured source.
            if let Some(built) =
                crate::native::build(build_project, &bound).map_err(|p| p.to_string())?
            {
                let generated = stage.join("snapback").join("generated");
                std::fs::create_dir_all(&generated).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
                std::fs::copy(&built.path, generated.join("native.wasm"))
                    .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            }
            std::fs::write(stage.join("device-compatibility.json"), serde_json::to_vec_pretty(&snapback4_core::device_protocol::compatibility(&bound.schema.public_projection())).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            crate::dev_worker::package_entry(build_project, &stage)?;
            if let Some(previous)=previous {
                if held["release"].as_u64()==Some(previous) && crate::release_files::digest(&stage)?==crate::release_files::digest(&releases.join(previous.to_string()))? {
                    std::fs::remove_dir_all(&stage).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
                    if let Some(saved) = &mut saved_version { saved.finish(previous,&source_hash)?; }
                    if options.json {println!("{}",serde_json::json!({"unchanged":true,"release":previous,"generation":held["generation"]}));}
                    else {println!("unchanged; release {previous} stays current as generation {}",held["generation"]);}
                    return Ok(());
                }
            }
            // @ref LLP 3000.000.000#16-release-reactivation-and-rollback-foresight-rel5 — equal retained executable bytes select their existing identity.
            let staged_digest = crate::release_files::digest(&stage)?;
            let mut matching = std::fs::read_dir(&releases).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?
                .filter_map(Result::ok).filter_map(|e| e.file_name().to_str()?.parse::<u64>().ok())
                .filter(|n| selectable(&dir, *n).is_ok())
                .collect::<Vec<_>>();
            matching.sort_unstable_by(|a,b| b.cmp(a));
            let mut matched = None;
            for n in matching {
                if crate::release_files::digest(&releases.join(n.to_string()))? == staged_digest { matched=Some(n); break; }
            }
            if let Some(n) = matched {
                let mut preview_options=options.clone();
                preview_options.flags.insert("deploy-direction".into(),String::new());
                preview_options.flags.insert("restore-dormant".into(),String::new());
                if !checking { preview_options.flags.insert("validation-only".into(),String::new()); }
                super::release_preview::preview(&dir.join("store.sqlite"),releases.join(n.to_string()),n,&preview_options)?;
                if checking { return Ok(()); }
                let selected = bound_at(releases.join(n.to_string()))?;
                activate(&dir,_deployment.as_ref(),n,previous,revision,selected,false,true,options.flags.contains_key("allow-feed-adoption"),options,None)?;
                if let Some(saved)=&mut saved_version {saved.finish(n,&source_hash)?;}
                return Ok(());
            }
            let mut n=std::fs::read_dir(&releases).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.filter_map(Result::ok).filter_map(|e|e.file_name().to_str()?.parse::<u64>().ok()).max().unwrap_or(0);
            let staged_bound = bound_at(stage.clone())?;
            if options.flags.contains_key("check") {
                let mut preview_options=options.clone();preview_options.flags.insert("deploy-direction".into(),String::new());
                let result=super::release_preview::preview(&dir.join("store.sqlite"),stage.clone(),n.checked_add(1).ok_or("release identity exhausted")?,&preview_options);
                let _=std::fs::remove_dir_all(&stage);
                return result;
            }
            let mut validation=options.clone();
            validation.flags.insert("deploy-direction".into(),String::new());
            validation.flags.insert("validation-only".into(),String::new());
            super::release_preview::preview(&dir.join("store.sqlite"),stage.clone(),n.checked_add(1).ok_or("release identity exhausted")?,&validation)?;
            if staged_bound.schema != bound.schema { return Err("staged source does not match the compiled schema".into()); }
            n=n.checked_add(1).ok_or("release identity exhausted")?;
            let final_dir=releases.join(n.to_string());
            // @ref LLP 3000.000.000#10-wave-10-release-lifecycle-amendment — only committed adoption makes a candidate selectable.
            std::fs::write(stage.join(".pending-adoption"), b"pending\n").map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
            if staged_bound.schema != bound.schema {
                return Err("staged source does not match the compiled schema".into());
            }
            // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.000.000#4-cli-surface-contracts — provenance is immutable metadata, excluded from executable identity so identical deploys stay no-ops.
            std::fs::write(stage.join("provenance.json"), format!("{}\n", serde_json::json!({
                "release": n, "sourceHash": source_hash, "cliVersion": env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION"), "createdAt": snapback4_core::now_ms()
            }))).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
            crate::release_files::durable(&stage)?;
            std::fs::rename(&stage, &final_dir).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            let mut candidate=crate::release_files::Candidate::new(&dir,n);
            std::fs::File::open(&releases)
                .and_then(|f| f.sync_all())
                .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            let result=activate(
                &dir,
                _deployment.as_ref(),
                n,
                previous,
                revision,
                staged_bound,
                false,
                options.flags.contains_key("restore-dormant"),
                options.flags.contains_key("allow-feed-adoption"),
                options,
                Some(&mut candidate),
            );
            match result {
                Ok(()) => {
                    candidate.committed=true;
                    if let Err(error)=std::fs::remove_file(final_dir.join(".pending-adoption")) {eprintln!("release {n} committed; pending-marker cleanup failed: {error}; history proves adoption");}
                    if let Some(saved) = &mut saved_version {saved.finish(n,&source_hash).map_err(|e|format!("release {n} committed, but saving its snapshot failed: {e}; retry unchanged deploy with --save-version"))?;}
                }
                Err(error) => {
                    if options.json && error.contains("dormant names") {
                        let mut inventory=Vec::new();let mut cursor=None;
                        loop {
                            let page=status(&dir,_deployment.as_ref(),0,cursor,None)?;
                            let items=page["items"].as_array().ok_or("invalid dormant inventory response")?;
                            if items.is_empty() {break;}
                            inventory.extend(items.iter().cloned());
                            cursor=items.last().and_then(|i|i["key"].as_str()).map(str::to_owned);
                            if cursor.is_none() {break;}
                        }
                        println!("{}",serde_json::json!({"ok":false,"code":"E_ADOPT","message":error,"dormant":inventory}));
                    }
                    return Err(error);
                }
            }
            Ok(())
        })(),
    )
}

pub fn rollback(options: &Options) -> i32 {
    report(
        "rollback",
        (|| {
            let dir = existing_deployment(&directory(options)?)?;
            let _command = command_lock(&dir)?;
            let _deployment = target(&dir)?;
            if let Some(owner) = &_deployment { owner.report_reclaim(options.flags.contains_key("verbose")); }
            let held = status(&dir, _deployment.as_ref(), 0, None, None)?;
            let active = if let Some(owner) = _deployment.as_ref() {
                current(&dir, owner)?
            } else {
                held["release"].as_u64()
            }
            .ok_or("no current release")?;
            let revision = held["revision"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0);
            let selected = options
                .flags
                .get("to")
                .map(|n| {
                    if n=="latest" {return latest(&dir).map_err(|_| "no complete latest release");}
                    n.parse::<u64>()
                        .ok()
                        .filter(|n| *n > 0)
                        .ok_or("--to requires a positive immutable release id")
                })
                .transpose()?;
            let previous = selected
                .or_else(|| held["previous_active"].as_u64())
                .ok_or("nothing to roll back to")?;
            selectable(&dir, previous)?;
            let bound = bound_at(dir.join("releases").join(previous.to_string()))?;
            // @ref LLP 3000.000.000#14-wave-14-rollback-narration — resolve bare/latest selection visibly before any preview or live activation.
            let target_line=format!("resolved rollback target: release {previous} (current release {active})");
            if options.json {eprintln!("{target_line}");} else {println!("{target_line}");}
            check_change(&dir, Some(active), &bound, options, Some(previous))?;
            if options.flags.contains_key("check") {
                return super::release_preview::preview(&dir.join("store.sqlite"),dir.join("releases").join(previous.to_string()),previous,options);
            }
            activate(
                &dir,
                _deployment.as_ref(),
                previous,
                Some(active),
                revision,
                bound,
                true,
                true,
                options.flags.contains_key("allow-feed-adoption"),
                options,
                None,
            )?;

            Ok(())
        })(),
    )
}

//! Operator preflight runs the real activation on a private disk-backed snapshot.
// REL3 owns dormant-visibility counts here; SURF clause narration lives in release_narration.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#92-exact-preview-and-operator-vocabulary — no live pointer, generation or audit changes.
use super::*;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
struct Snapshot(PathBuf);
impl Drop for Snapshot {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&self.0);
    }
}

pub(super) fn preview(
    store_path: &Path,
    root: PathBuf,
    release: u64,
    options: &Options,
) -> Result<(), String> {
    let bound = super::releases::bound_at(root.clone())?;
    let project = Project { root };
    let native = crate::native::prepare(&project, &bound).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    crate::dev_worker::prepare_release(&project,&bound.schema)?;
    let id = snapback4_core::ids::mint(snapback4_core::now_ms(), &mut snapback4_core::ids::Mint::default())
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let snapshot = Snapshot(std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("snapback4-release-preview-{id}")));
    snapback4_core::private_fs::directory(&snapshot.0).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let path = snapshot.0.join("store.sqlite");
    // SQLite VACUUM INTO takes a coherent read snapshot even with a live WAL writer.
    // Busy/disk errors refuse this preview; never fall back to copying database bytes.
    if store_path.exists() {
    let source = snapback4_core::Store::open_read_only(store_path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    source
        .conn()
        .execute("VACUUM INTO ?1", [path.to_string_lossy().as_ref()])
        .map_err(|e| {
            format!("snapshot preview failed (retry after store/disk becomes available): {e}")
        })?;
    drop(source);
    }
    let mut store = snapback4_core::Store::open(&path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let held = store
        .release_status(0, None, None)
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let mut activation =
        snapback4_core::engine::release::ReleaseActivation::new(release, held["release"].as_u64());
    activation.expected_revision = held["revision"].as_u64();
    activation.native_digest = native.installed.map(|n| n.digest);
    activation.operation = "preview".into();
    activation.restore_dormant = !options.flags.contains_key("deploy-direction") || options.flags.contains_key("restore-dormant");
    let mut steps = Vec::new();
    let estimate = store
        .adoption_estimate(&bound.schema)
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?
        .map(|e| e.summary());
    let mut client_compatibility=Vec::new();
    if let Some(current)=held["release"].as_u64() {
        let prior=super::releases::bound_at(store_path.parent().ok_or("no deployment directory")?.join("releases").join(current.to_string()))?;
        client_compatibility=snapback4_lang::client_contracts::compare(&prior,&bound,&format!("release {current}"));
    }
    let candidate_schema=bound.schema.clone();
    let candidate_programs:std::collections::BTreeMap<_,_>=bound.programs.iter().map(|p|(p.program.name.clone(),p.program.clone())).collect();
    let mut hazards=Vec::new();
    let mut notes=Vec::new();
    let prior:Option<(String,String)>=store.conn().query_row("SELECT schema,programs FROM _generations WHERE generation=(SELECT CAST(value AS INTEGER) FROM _meta WHERE key='generation')",[],|r|Ok((r.get(0)?,r.get(1)?))).ok();
    let prior_schema=prior.as_ref().map(|(schema,_)|serde_json::from_str::<snapback4_core::Schema>(schema).map_err(|e|e.to_string())).transpose()?;
    if let Some((schema,programs))=prior {
        let prior:snapback4_core::Schema=serde_json::from_str(&schema).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
        steps=snapback4_core::successor::classify(&prior,&candidate_schema);
        let programs:std::collections::BTreeMap<String,snapback4_core::ir::Program>=serde_json::from_str(&programs).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
        let target_label = format!("release {release}");
        let compatibility = super::release_narration::compatibility(&store, &prior, &candidate_schema, held["release"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0), &target_label, options.flags.contains_key("deploy-direction"))?;
        for line in compatibility {
            if let Some(warning) = line.strip_prefix("WARNING ") { hazards.push(warning.to_owned()); }
            else { notes.push(line.trim_start_matches("note: ").to_owned()); }
        }
        let rollback=!options.flags.contains_key("deploy-direction");
        let (behavior_warnings, behavior_notes) = super::release_narration::behavior(store_path, &store, &prior, &candidate_schema, &programs, &candidate_programs, &project, held["release"].as_u64(), &target_label, rollback)?;
        hazards.extend(behavior_warnings); notes.extend(behavior_notes);
        for name in prior.tables.keys().filter(|name|!candidate_schema.tables.contains_key(*name) && prior.maintain_for(name).is_none() && prior.tables[*name].ephemeral.is_none()) {
            let count:u64=store.conn().query_row("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM r WHERE t=(SELECT num FROM _catalog WHERE name=?1)",[name],|r|r.get::<_,i64>(0).map(|n|n as u64)).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
            if count>0 {hazards.push(format!("table {name}: {count} rows become dormant; {target_label} lacks this table's behavior; preserve it in a bridge or accept its absence"));}
        }
    }
    super::release_safeguards::capture(&store)?;
    let mut engine = snapback4_core::Engine::new_release_prepared(
        store,
        bound.schema,
        bound.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program),
        &activation,
        options.flags.contains_key("allow-feed-adoption"),
    )
    .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let mut planned = engine
        .store_mut()
        .release_status(0, None, None)
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    planned["items"] = serde_json::to_value(
        engine
            .store_mut()
            .dormant_inventory(None, usize::MAX)
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?,
    )
    .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let items: Vec<snapback4_core::store::DormantItem> = serde_json::from_value(planned["items"].clone()).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let mut effects = super::release_safeguards::warnings(engine.store(),prior_schema.as_ref(),&candidate_schema,&candidate_programs,&items,release,!options.flags.contains_key("deploy-direction"))?;
    effects.append(&mut hazards); hazards=effects;
    for fill in &engine.store().adoption_report.backfills { notes.push(format!("backfill: {fill}; confirm these historical values before selecting release {release}")); }
    steps.retain(|step|!step.message.contains("engine capture"));
    for step in &mut steps {
        if step.change==snapback4_core::successor::Change::Narrowing {
            step.change=snapback4_core::successor::Change::Additive;
            step.message=format!("{}; active rows satisfy the candidate after dormant projection",step.message.split(';').next().unwrap_or(&step.message));
        }
        if let Some(position)=step.path.strip_prefix("maintains[").and_then(|s|s.strip_suffix(']')).and_then(|s|s.parse::<usize>().ok()) {
            let label=candidate_schema.maintains[position].label();
            let visits=engine.store().adoption_report.views.iter().find(|view|view.name==label).map(|v|v.rows).unwrap_or(0);
            step.message=format!("maintain {label}: rebuilt from {visits} source rows");
            step.change=snapback4_core::successor::Change::Additive;
        }
        let path=step.path.strip_prefix("tables.").unwrap_or(&step.path).replace(".columns.",".");
        let count=planned["items"].as_array().into_iter().flatten().find(|i|i["path"].as_str()==Some(path.as_str())).and_then(|i|i["rows"].as_u64()).unwrap_or(0);
        let unit = if step.path.contains(".columns.") { "cells" } else if step.path.contains(".indexes.") { "entries" } else { "rows" };
        // @ref LLP 3000.000.000#11-wave-11-factual-release-narration — per-path prose uses the same disjoint counts as the inventory totals.
        let retained = if count == 0 && unit == "cells" {
            format!("no standalone {unit} become dormant (cells of any dormant rows are counted with those rows)")
        } else if count == 0 {
            format!("no {unit} become dormant")
        } else {
            format!("its {unit} become dormant ({count} {unit}; restored on forward)")
        };
        step.message=step.message.replace("its rows are lost", &retained).replace("its values are lost", &retained);
    }
    if options.flags.contains_key("deploy-direction") {
        if let Some(current) = held["release"].as_u64() {
            let old_root=store_path.parent().ok_or("no deployment directory")?.join("releases").join(current.to_string());
            let old=super::releases::bound_at(old_root.clone())?;
            hazards.extend(super::rollback_foresight::risks(&candidate_schema,&old.schema,current,store_path.parent().unwrap()));
            // Exact reverse adoption tests today's post-deploy image, separately from future-write risks.
            let reverse_path=snapshot.0.join("reverse.sqlite");
            engine.store().conn().execute("VACUUM INTO ?1",[reverse_path.to_string_lossy().as_ref()]).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
            let reverse_store=snapback4_core::Store::open(&reverse_path).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
            let native=crate::native::prepare(&Project {root:old_root},&old).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
            let mut reverse=snapback4_core::engine::release::ReleaseActivation::new(current,Some(release));
            reverse.restore_dormant=true;
            reverse.native_digest=native.installed.map(|m|m.digest);
            reverse.operation="preview".into();
            if let Err(error)=snapback4_core::Engine::new_release_prepared(reverse_store,old.schema,old.programs.into_iter().map(|p|p.program),&reverse,options.flags.contains_key("allow-feed-adoption")) {
                hazards.push(format!("rollback to release {current} refuses on the post-deploy snapshot: {error}; run `snapback4 rollback {} --to {current} --check --bridge <new-dir>` and preserve the required fields/domains in a bridge",store_path.parent().unwrap().display()));
            }
        }
    }
    hazards.extend(client_compatibility.iter().cloned());
    if options.flags.contains_key("validation-only") {
        for hazard in &hazards { eprintln!("WARNING {hazard}"); }
        return Ok(());
    }
    let checklist = super::release_bridge::checklist(held["release"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0),release,&hazards);
    if let Some(path) = options.flags.get("bridge") {
        let current = held["release"].as_u64().ok_or("a bridge needs a current deployed release")?;
        super::release_bridge::write(Path::new(path),store_path.parent().ok_or("no deployment directory")?,current,release,&checklist)?;
        eprintln!("bridge starter written to {path}; edit its current source and follow ROLLBACK-CHECKLIST.md before activation");
    }
    if options.json {
        println!("{}",serde_json::to_string_pretty(&serde_json::json!({"preview":true,"clientCompatibility":client_compatibility,"checklist":checklist,"hazards":hazards,"notes":notes,"current":held,"plan":planned,"steps":steps,"estimate":estimate,"phases":engine.store().adoption_report.summary(),"backfills":engine.store().adoption_report.backfills})).unwrap());
    } else {
        println!("preview: release {release} would be current as generation {}; live release unchanged", engine.generation());
        super::release_summary::print(prior_schema.as_ref(), &candidate_schema, &candidate_programs, &hazards, &notes, &steps, &items);
        if !options.flags.contains_key("deploy-direction") { println!("  checklist: prove the named refusal cases and affected lists/counts as real accounts before release {release}; `--bridge <new-dir>` saves current source plus this app's ROLLBACK-CHECKLIST.md"); }
        println!("  storage: {}",snapback4_core::store::adoption_report::AdoptionReport::dormant(&items));
        if options.flags.contains_key("verbose") {
            for hazard in hazards { println!("  detail: WARNING {hazard}"); }
            for note in notes { println!("  detail: note: {note}"); }
            for step in steps {println!("  {}: {}",step.path,step.message);}
            for item in &items { println!("  {}: {} {} dormant (restored on compatible forward); sample ids [{}]", item.key, item.rows, item.unit(), item.sample_ids.join(", ")); }
            println!("{}", engine.store().adoption_report.summary());
            if let Some(estimate) = estimate {
                println!("{estimate}");
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}
//! Immutable release custody: digest the required tree and flush it before DB selection.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#5-hot-activation-and-ownership — source durability precedes the database's release pointer.
use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
use std::{
    fs,
    io::{Read, Write},
    path::{Path, PathBuf},
};

fn files(root: &Path, path: &Path, out: &mut Vec<PathBuf>) -> Result<(), String> {
    let mut entries = fs::read_dir(path)
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?
        .map(|e| e.map(|e| e.path()).map_err(|e| e.to_string()))
        .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()?;
    entries.sort();
    for path in entries {
        let relative = path.strip_prefix(root).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        if relative
            .components()
            .next()
            .is_some_and(|c| c.as_os_str() == ".snapback4")
        {
            continue;
        }
        let m = fs::symlink_metadata(&path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        if m.is_dir() {
            files(root, &path, out)?;
        } else if m.is_file() {
            out.push(path);
        } else {
            return Err(format!(
                "immutable release artifact must be a real directory or regular file: {}",
                path.display()
            ));
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}
pub(crate) fn digest(root: &Path) -> Result<String, String> {
    let mut paths = Vec::new();
    files(root, root, &mut paths)?;
    let mut hash = Sha256::new();
    for path in paths {
        let name = path.strip_prefix(root).unwrap().to_string_lossy();
        // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.000.000#4-cli-surface-contracts — metadata does not change executable release identity.
        if name == ".pending-adoption" || name == "provenance.json" {continue;}
        hash.update((name.len() as u64).to_le_bytes());
        hash.update(name.as_bytes());
        #[cfg(unix)]
        use std::os::unix::fs::OpenOptionsExt;
        let mut options = fs::OpenOptions::new();
        options.read(true);
        #[cfg(unix)]
        options.custom_flags(libc::O_NOFOLLOW);
        let mut file = options.open(&path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        hash.update(
            file.metadata()
                .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?
                .len()
                .to_le_bytes(),
        );
        let mut buffer = [0u8; 16384];
        loop {
            let n = file.read(&mut buffer).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            if n == 0 {
                break;
            }
            hash.update(&buffer[..n]);
        }
    }
    Ok(format!("{:x}", hash.finalize()))
}
pub(crate) fn durable(root: &Path) -> Result<(), String> {
    let mut paths = Vec::new();
    files(root, root, &mut paths)?;
    for path in paths {
        fs::File::open(path)
            .and_then(|f| f.sync_all())
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    }
    fn dirs(path: &Path) -> Result<(), String> {
        for e in fs::read_dir(path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())? {
            let e = e.map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            if e.file_type().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?.is_dir() {
                dirs(&e.path())?;
            }
        }
        fs::File::open(path)
            .and_then(|f| f.sync_all())
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())
    }
    dirs(root)
}

pub(crate) fn link_store(root: &Path) -> Result<(), String> {
    let project = crate::project::Project { root: root.into() };
    snapback4_core::private_fs::directory(&project.data_dir()).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let path = project.store_path();
    #[cfg(unix)]
    {
        let target = Path::new("../../../store.sqlite");
        match fs::symlink_metadata(&path) {
            Ok(m) if m.file_type().is_symlink() => {
                if fs::read_link(&path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())? != target {
                    fs::remove_file(&path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
                    std::os::unix::fs::symlink(target, &path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
                }
            }
            Ok(_) => {
                return Err(format!(
                    "release store path must be a symbolic link: {}",
                    path.display()
                ));
            }
            Err(e) if e.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => {
                std::os::unix::fs::symlink(target, &path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?
            }
            Err(e) => return Err(e.to_string()),
        }
        Ok(())
    }
    #[cfg(not(unix))]
    Err("serving shared releases requires Unix symbolic links".into())
}

pub(crate) struct Pointer {
    staged: PathBuf,
    dir: PathBuf,
    release: u64,
    previous: Option<u64>,
    rollback: bool,
    ledger: fs::File,
}
impl Pointer {
    pub(crate) fn prepare(
        dir: &Path,
        release: u64,
        previous: Option<u64>,
        rollback: bool,
    ) -> Result<Self, String> {
        let pointer = dir.join("current");
        if pointer.exists() && !pointer.is_file() {
            return Err("current must be a regular release pointer file".into());
        }
        let staged = dir.join(format!(".current-{}-{release}", std::process::id()));
        let file = snapback4_core::private_fs::file(&staged, true).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let mut prepared = Self {
            staged,
            dir: dir.into(),
            release,
            previous,
            rollback,
            ledger: file,
        };
        writeln!(prepared.ledger, "{release}")
            .and_then(|_| prepared.ledger.sync_all())
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        prepared.ledger = fs::OpenOptions::new()
            .create(true)
            .append(true)
            .open(dir.join("releases.jsonl"))
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        writeln!(prepared.ledger,"{}",serde_json::json!({"at":snapback4_core::now_ms(),"release":release,"previous":previous,"rollback":rollback,"state":"prepared"})).and_then(|_|prepared.ledger.sync_all()).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
        Ok(prepared)
    }
    pub(crate) fn finish(&mut self, generation: u64) -> Result<(), String> {
        fs::rename(&self.staged,self.dir.join("current")).and_then(|_|fs::File::open(&self.dir)?.sync_all()).map_err(|e|format!("release {} committed in database, but current copy failed: {e}; inspect `snapback4 releases {}`",self.release,self.dir.display()))?;
        writeln!(self.ledger,"{}",serde_json::json!({"at":snapback4_core::now_ms(),"release":self.release,"previous":self.previous,"rollback":self.rollback,"generation":generation,"state":"activated"})).and_then(|_|self.ledger.sync_all()).map_err(|e|format!("release committed, but ledger completion failed: {e}"))
    }
}
impl Drop for Pointer {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        if self.staged.exists() {
            let _ = fs::remove_file(&self.staged);
        }
    }
}

        let (mut uid, mut gid) = (0, 0);

#[cfg(unix)]
fn socket_entry(path: &Path) -> io::Result<fs::Metadata> {
    let m = fs::symlink_metadata(path)?;
    if !m.file_type().is_socket()
        || m.uid() != unsafe { libc::geteuid() }
        || m.permissions().mode() & 0o7777 != 0o600
    {
        return Err(io::Error::other(
            "release control must be a same-owner 0600 socket, never a symlink or regular file",
        ));
    }
    Ok(m)
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#51-path-independent-control-address — fixed short runtime root, independent of shell environment.
#[cfg(unix)]
fn private_directory(path: &Path, create: bool) -> io::Result<()> {
    if create {
        match fs::DirBuilder::new().mode(0o700).create(path) {
            Ok(()) => {}
            Err(e) if e.kind() == io::ErrorKind::AlreadyExists => {}
            Err(e) => return Err(e),
        }
    }
    // Do not chmod or follow an existing entry. A stranger can pre-create a
    // name in /tmp, but cannot turn that into a capability or chmod target.
    let directory = fs::OpenOptions::new()
        .read(true)
        .custom_flags(libc::O_DIRECTORY | libc::O_NOFOLLOW)
        .open(path)?;
    directory_metadata(&directory.metadata()?, unsafe { libc::geteuid() })
}

#[cfg(unix)]
fn directory_metadata(m: &fs::Metadata, uid: u32) -> io::Result<()> {
    if !m.is_dir() || m.uid() != uid || m.permissions().mode() & 0o7777 != 0o700 {
        return Err(io::Error::other(
            "release control runtime directory must be a same-owner 0700 directory",
        ));
    }
    Ok(())
}

#[cfg(unix)]
fn address(dir: &Path, create: bool) -> io::Result<PathBuf> {
    let directory = PathBuf::from(format!("/tmp/snapback4-{}", unsafe { libc::geteuid() }));
    private_directory(&directory, create)?;
    let canonical = dir.canonicalize()?;
    // Bind the address to immutable deployment source, even when two
    // deployments symlink their database to the same store. The actual
    // database path/inode is separately checked in every envelope.
    Ok(directory.join(format!(
        "{}.sock",
        crate::native::digest(canonical.as_os_str().as_bytes())
    )))
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#51-path-independent-control-address — a total input deadline bounds shutdown even when a peer keeps sending bytes.
#[cfg(unix)]
fn input(stream: &mut UnixStream, timeout: Duration) -> io::Result<Vec<u8>> {
    let deadline = Instant::now() + timeout;
    let mut bytes = Vec::new();
    let mut buffer = [0; INPUT + 1];
    while bytes.len() <= INPUT {
        let remaining = deadline.saturating_duration_since(Instant::now());
        if remaining.is_zero() {
            return Err(io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::TimedOut,
                "release control input deadline exceeded",
            ));
        }
        stream.set_read_timeout(Some(remaining))?;
        match stream.read(&mut buffer[..INPUT + 1 - bytes.len()]) {
            Ok(0) => break,
            Ok(n) => bytes.extend_from_slice(&buffer[..n]),
            Err(e) if e.kind() == io::ErrorKind::Interrupted => continue,
            Err(e) => return Err(e),
        }
    }
    Ok(bytes)
}

/// Only called while the exclusive database sidecar is held, so stale socket
/// replacement cannot disconnect another admitted serving owner.
#[cfg(unix)]
pub(crate) struct Listener {
    listener: UnixListener,
    path: PathBuf,
    inode: (u64, u64),
    identity: String,
}
#[cfg(unix)]
impl Listener {
    pub(crate) fn bind(dir: &Path, store: &Path) -> Result<Self, String> {
        let identity = identity(store)?;
        let path = address(dir, true).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        match fs::symlink_metadata(&path) {
            Ok(_) => {
                socket_entry(&path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
                match UnixStream::connect(&path) {
                    Ok(_) => {
                        return Err("release control socket already has a live listener".into());
                    }
                    Err(e)
                        if matches!(
                            e.kind(),
                            io::ErrorKind::ConnectionRefused | io::ErrorKind::NotFound
                        ) => {}
                    Err(e) => return Err(e.to_string()),
                }
                fs::remove_file(&path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            }
            Err(e) if e.kind() == io::ErrorKind::NotFound => {}
            Err(e) => return Err(e.to_string()),
        }
        let listener = UnixListener::bind(&path)
            .map_err(|e| format!("release control {}: {e}", path.display()))?;
        let m = fs::symlink_metadata(&path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let owned = Self {
            listener,
            path,
            inode: (m.dev(), m.ino()),
            identity,
        };
        // Establish cleanup custody before any fallible setup after bind.
        fs::set_permissions(&owned.path, fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o600))
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        owned
            .listener
            .set_nonblocking(true)
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        socket_entry(&owned.path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        Ok(owned)
    }
    fn accept(&self) -> io::Result<UnixStream> {
        let (stream, _) = self.listener.accept()?;
        // Darwin inherits O_NONBLOCK from the listener; input's deadline and
        // socket timeouts require blocking I/O, even before the client writes.
        stream.set_nonblocking(false)?;
        Ok(stream)
    }
    fn handle(
        &self,
        stream: &mut UnixStream,
        mut handle: impl FnMut(Command) -> Result<Value, String>,
    ) {
        let result = (|| -> Result<Value, String> {
            peer(&stream).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            stream
                .set_write_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(5)))
                .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            let bytes = input(stream, Duration::from_secs(5)).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            if bytes.len() > INPUT {
                return Err("release control command exceeds 4096 bytes".into());
            }
            let request: Envelope = serde_json::from_slice(&bytes).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            if request.identity != self.identity {
                return Err("release control store identity differs; run releases against the owned deployment".into());
            }
            handle(request.command)
        })();
        let reply = match result {
            Ok(value) => json!({"ok":value}),
            Err(error) => json!({"error":error}),
        };
        let mut bytes = serde_json::to_vec(&reply).unwrap();
        if bytes.len() > OUTPUT {
            bytes=serde_json::to_vec(&json!({"error":"release control reply exceeds its bound; inspect paginated `snapback4 releases <dir>`; an admitted activation/prune may already have committed"})).unwrap();
        }
        // A timeout/lost peer does not cancel or undo admitted work.
        let _ = stream.write_all(&bytes);
    }
    pub(crate) fn run(self, mut handle: impl FnMut(Command) -> Result<Value, String>) {
        while !crate::lifecycle::stopping() {
            match self.accept() {
                Ok(mut stream) => {
                    self.handle(&mut stream, &mut handle);
                }
                Err(e) if e.kind() == io::ErrorKind::WouldBlock => {
                    std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(25))
                }
                Err(e) => {
                    snapback4_core::diagnostic!("release control accept: {e}");
                    std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(50));
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

diff --git a/snapback4/crates/snapback4-core/src/store/dormant_tests.rs b/snapback4/crates/snapback4-core/src/store/dormant_tests.rs
index 12eb3c24aa..7dc54324cb 100644
--- a/snapback4/crates/snapback4-core/src/store/dormant_tests.rs
+++ b/snapback4/crates/snapback4-core/src/store/dormant_tests.rs
@@ -1377,3 +1377,19 @@ fn restoration_conflict_samples_keep_distinct_duplicate_keys_separate() {
     assert!(!error.contains("active rows [a1") && !error.contains("active rows [a2"),"{error}");
     assert_eq!(e.store().count("rows").unwrap(),2);
 }
+
+#[test]
+fn compiler_intent_indexes_do_not_require_authored_restoration_but_authored_indexes_do() {
+    let indexed=V1.replace("  n: int","  by byNumber: n, id\n  n: int");
+    for (name,expected) in [("byNumber",false),("$intent:rel5",true)] {
+        let (mut s,p)=compile(&indexed);
+        let table=s.tables.get_mut("rows").unwrap();
+        let index=table.indexes.remove("byNumber").unwrap();table.indexes.insert(name.into(),index);
+        let mut e=Engine::new_release(Store::memory().unwrap(),s.clone(),p.clone(),1,None).unwrap();
+        activate(&mut e,V1,2).unwrap();
+        let options=ReleaseActivation{restore_dormant:false,..ReleaseActivation::new(3,Some(2))};
+        let result=e.adopt_release(s,p,&options,false);
+        assert_eq!(result.is_ok(),expected,"{name}: {result:?}");
+        if !expected {assert!(result.unwrap_err().to_string().contains("restoration intent"));}
+    }
+}

 ## Trigger a deployed job
 A running serve accepts `POST /jobs/<job>` with a full service token; the job executes as the system on its FIFO queue and server clock. Every full service seat can trigger system jobs, so provision these tokens only to trusted operators. Guest/account sessions, read-scoped tokens and personas cannot use this door. `POST /dev/jobs/<job>` remains development-only.

 The new versions/v1 captures selected modules, their contemporary fixtures and `versions/v1/release.json` with the actual immutable release id, source hash and CLI version. Unchanged deploy keeps its id; existing snapshots never change. Choose a new positive-numbered versions/vN; saving cannot combine with --check. Saved-source deploy uses its own fixtures. Use the recorded id when choosing a baseline; since_release remains the author-supplied newer-schema label. The bridge starts from complete current Quarry source and this app's `bridge/ROLLBACK-CHECKLIST.md`. Retain current domains, audiences, guards and quotas; repair records through authorized mutations; prove blocked cases refuse and affected lists/counts remain correct; repeat the old-source preview. No bridge is activated or business migration synthesized. JSON includes checklist; --verbose retains full evidence/index paths. Dormant rows reserve ids but no unique slots: new active rows may block restoration. A widened non-unique bridge permits repair before reinstating uniqueness; retained storage cannot supply absent safety logic.
+
+## Keep v1 clients working
+
+`deploy --check --json` reports `clientCompatibility` separately from data
+adoption: missing operations, changed kinds, required new arguments, removed or
+narrowed inputs, result shape/value changes and changed refusal codes/messages.
+Deploy prints the same warnings. Input breaks name the old release and the
+E_INPUT/E_OP outcome. A result or behavior review asks you to verify the intended
+contract; a correct new privacy denial is intentional behavior, not an input
+shape failure. The comparison is conservative, not a proof of unchanged logic.
+
+For example, save this v1 source and its callers sending
+`{name:"Ada",obsolete:"old input"}` to `greet`:
+
+```quarry
+use identity guests
+query greet(name: text <=40, obsolete: text <=40):
+  return {message: name + '!'}
+```
+
+Deploy this v2 source. The unchanged v1 call still returns `{message:"Ada!"}`;
+new callers can use `greeting` and omit `suffix` and `obsolete`:
+
+```quarry
+use identity guests
+query greeting(name: text <=40, suffix: text <=1 default '!', obsolete: text <=40 deprecated):
+  return {message: name + suffix}
+alias greet = greeting
+```
+
+A literal default handles omitted or null arguments and must fit the declared
+scalar type; explicit non-null input still validates. `deprecated` accepts a
+validated optional argument but forbids referring to it in the body. Unknown
+arguments still refuse. These modifiers apply to non-native Quarry queries and
+mutations; helpers, actions and jobs keep their distinct invocation contracts.
+Aliases target an authored query/mutation in the same module, carry the same
+rules/guards/results, and cannot chain. Retain the old endpoint until its callers
+have retired; preserve the old result projection and handle intended denials.
+
+Deploy also warns when later rollback can refuse or hide rows: dropping a
+required old column or widening a value domain can strand newly written rows.
+The preflight separately attempts exact reverse adoption on its post-deploy
+snapshot, naming present-data failures. Keep old fields and compatible domains
+in an expand/contract bridge; after writes run the named
+`rollback <deployment> --to N --check --bridge <new-dir>` before selecting N.
+A green snapshot never freezes later writes.

 //! Compiled examples first; diagnostic details are under `why`.
 pub const LANGUAGE: &str = r##"# Quarry — the language card

diff --git a/snapback4/packages/snapback4/CHANGELOG.md b/snapback4/packages/snapback4/CHANGELOG.md
index fcf3bc679a..6eb7af69b1 100644
--- a/snapback4/packages/snapback4/CHANGELOG.md
+++ b/snapback4/packages/snapback4/CHANGELOG.md
@@ -1,5 +1,9 @@
 # Snapback 4 behavior changes
 
+## 0.4.34
+
+- Guide changes: `guide deploy` explains retained-source reactivation with the same release id, atomic refused-candidate cleanup, the shared `--restore-dormant` intent and deploy-time rollback foresight with bridge commands. Old-client compatibility reports compare operation inputs, results and refusals; required new inputs remain required in generated types. The executable v1/v2 recipe uses literal query/mutation defaults, ignored deprecated inputs and same-module operation aliases. Availability accepts extra optional served arguments. Migration recipes and staging clock changes are owned by the data lifecycle workstream; security invariants/audit by the security workstream.
+
 ## 0.4.33
 
 - Declare independent security invariants beside access rules. `check` reports proved, refuted with a replayable counterexample, or unproved. Supported local rule implications, typed aggregate contributor audiences and exact server lifetime admission have scoped proofs; general temporal histories, opaque writers, capture/live-parent reductions and disconnected-device freshness remain explicitly unproved. Passing finite challenges is not a universal security proof.

diff --git a/snapback4/packages/snapback4/test/release-transitions.test.mjs b/snapback4/packages/snapback4/test/release-transitions.test.mjs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000..8c6f5cdcfa
--- /dev/null
+++ b/snapback4/packages/snapback4/test/release-transitions.test.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,11 @@
+import test from 'node:test';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import { cli, startServe } from '../dist/proof.js';
+import { releaseTransitions } from '../../../scripts/release-transitions-cases.mjs';
+test('release reactivation, refusal cleanup, rollback foresight and frozen v1 guide callers', {timeout:180_000}, async () => {
+  const original=process.env.SNAPBACK4_BIN;
+  process.env.SNAPBACK4_BIN??=fileURLToPath(new URL('../../../target/debug/snapback4',import.meta.url));
+  try {await releaseTransitions({cli,startServe},tmpdir());}
+  finally {if(original===undefined)delete process.env.SNAPBACK4_BIN;else process.env.SNAPBACK4_BIN=original;}
+});
diff --git a/snapback4/scripts/publication-release-transitions.mjs b/snapback4/scripts/publication-release-transitions.mjs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000..ad420a7b3a
--- /dev/null
+++ b/snapback4/scripts/publication-release-transitions.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,13 @@
+// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.000#11-old-client-operation-contracts-rel5 — the installed guide is the source exercised by packed qualification.
+import {readFileSync, writeFileSync, rmSync} from 'node:fs';
+import {join} from 'node:path';
+import {runToolchainCommand} from './publication-starters.mjs';
+export async function proveReleaseTransitions(cwd) {
+  const path=join(cwd,'release-transitions.mjs');
+  const source=readFileSync(new URL('./release-transitions-cases.mjs',import.meta.url),'utf8');
+  writeFileSync(path,source+`\nimport {cli,startServe} from 'snapback4/proof';\nconsole.log(JSON.stringify(await releaseTransitions({cli,startServe},process.cwd())));\n`);
+  const env={...process.env};delete env.SNAPBACK4_BIN;delete env.PORT_LO;delete env.PORT_HI;
+  try {await runToolchainCommand(process.execPath,[path],{cwd,env,timeout:240_000});}
+  finally {rmSync(path,{force:true});}
+  return {status:'passed',cases:['reactivation','refused-deploy-cleanliness','rollback-foresight','old-client-diff','installed-v1-v2-guide']};
+}

diff --git a/snapback4/scripts/release-transitions-cases.mjs b/snapback4/scripts/release-transitions-cases.mjs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000..15ed211030
--- /dev/null
+++ b/snapback4/scripts/release-transitions-cases.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,128 @@
+// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#16-release-reactivation-and-rollback-foresight-rel5 — run the same transitions with development and installed binaries.
+import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
+import { mkdirSync, writeFileSync, readFileSync, readdirSync, mkdtempSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { join } from 'node:path';
+
+export async function releaseTransitions({ cli, startServe }, parent) {
+  const root=mkdtempSync(join(parent,'release-transitions-'));
+  const active=new Set();
+  try {
+    const fixture=name=> {
+      const cwd=join(root,name);mkdirSync(join(cwd,'snapback'),{recursive:true});
+      const deployment=join(cwd,'deployment'),schema=join(cwd,'snapback/schema.q');
+      const invoke=args=>cli(args,{cwd,timeoutMs:60_000});
+      const run=async args=>{const r=await invoke(args);assert.equal(r.code,0,r.stdout+r.stderr);return r;};
+      const status=async()=>JSON.parse((await run(['releases',deployment,'--json'])).stdout);
+      const releases=()=>readdirSync(join(deployment,'releases')).sort();
+      const source=text=>writeFileSync(schema,text);
+      const serve=async()=> {
+        const host=await startServe(deployment,{cwd,timeoutMs:60_000});active.add(host);
+        const signed=await (await fetch(host.url+'/auth/guest',{method:'POST',body:'{}'})).json();
+        assert.ok(signed.session?.token,JSON.stringify(signed));
+        const headers={'content-type':'application/json',authorization:'Bearer '+signed.session.token};
+        const post=async(path,args={},id)=> {
+          const r=await fetch(host.url+path,{method:'POST',headers,body:JSON.stringify({args,...(id?{id}:{})}),signal:AbortSignal.timeout(30_000)});
+          return r.json();
+        };
+        return {host,post};
+      };
+      return {cwd,deployment,schema,invoke,run,status,releases,source,serve};
+    };
+    const f=fixture('reactivation');
+    const v1=`use identity guests
+table items:
+  body: text <=40
+  public 'release proof'
+  insert <- allow
+  update <- allow
+mutation add(body: text <=40):
+  return insert items {body}
+query list():
+  return items first 20 by .id
+`;
+    const v2=v1.replace('  body: text','  extra: text <=40 default \'default\'\n  body: text')+`mutation mark(id: items):
+  return update items[id] {extra: 'preserved'}
+`;
+    f.source(v1);await f.run(['deploy',f.deployment]);
+    f.source(v2);await f.run(['deploy',f.deployment]);await f.run(['rollback',f.deployment,'--to','1']);
+    const before=await f.status();
+    const check=JSON.parse((await f.run(['deploy',f.deployment,'--check','--json'])).stdout);
+    assert.equal(check.plan.release,2);assert.deepEqual(await f.status(),before);assert.deepEqual(f.releases(),['1','2']);
+    assert.equal(JSON.parse((await f.run(['deploy',f.deployment,'--json'])).stdout).release,2);
+    const {host,post}=await f.serve();
+    const added=await post('/m/add',{body:'kept'},'rel5-add');assert.equal(added.state,'sent',JSON.stringify(added));
+    const id=added.result.id;
+    assert.equal((await post('/m/mark',{id},'rel5-mark')).state,'sent');
+    await f.run(['rollback',f.deployment,'--to','1','--restore-dormant']);
+    await f.run(['deploy',f.deployment]);
+    assert.equal((await f.status()).release,2);assert.equal((await post('/q/list')).data[0].extra,'preserved');
+    const unchanged=await f.status();assert.equal(JSON.parse((await f.run(['deploy',f.deployment,'--json'])).stdout).unchanged,true);
+    assert.deepEqual(await f.status(),unchanged);
+    f.source(v2.replace('  body: text','  required: int\n  body: text').replace('insert items {body}','insert items {body, required: 1}'));
+    const failed=await f.invoke(['deploy',f.deployment]);assert.equal(failed.code,1);assert.match(failed.stderr,/E_ADOPT/);
+    assert.deepEqual(f.releases(),['1','2']);assert.deepEqual(await f.status(),unchanged);
+    assert.equal((await post('/q/list')).data[0].extra,'preserved');
+    await host.stop();active.delete(host);
+    // Failure after validation, before database commit, also cleans its numeric source.
+    f.source(v2.replace("return items first 20", "return items first 19"));
+    rmSync(join(f.deployment,'current'));mkdirSync(join(f.deployment,'current'));
+    const pointerFailure=await f.invoke(['deploy',f.deployment]);assert.equal(pointerFailure.code,1);assert.match(pointerFailure.stderr,/current must be a regular/);
+    assert.deepEqual(f.releases(),['1','2']);rmSync(join(f.deployment,'current'),{recursive:true});writeFileSync(join(f.deployment,'current'),'2\n');
+
+    const g=fixture('foresight');
+    const prior=`use identity guests
+table gauges:
+  minPressure: int 0..2000
+  public 'rollback proof'
+  insert <- allow
+mutation add():
+  return insert gauges {minPressure: 4}
+`;
+    const next=prior.replace('  minPressure: int 0..2000\n','  lowPressure: int 0..2000 default 0\n').replace('{minPressure: 4}','{lowPressure: 4}');
+    g.source(prior);await g.run(['deploy',g.deployment]);g.source(next);
+    const foresight=JSON.parse((await g.run(['deploy',g.deployment,'--check','--json'])).stdout);
+    assert.ok(foresight.hazards.some(s=>s.includes('gauges.minPressure')&&s.includes('new rows')&&s.includes('--to 1 --check --bridge')));
+    assert.match((await g.run(['deploy',g.deployment])).stderr,/rollback foresight.*gauges.minPressure/);
+    const h=await g.serve();assert.equal((await h.post('/m/add',{},'rel5-new-gauge')).state,'sent');
+    const rollback=await g.invoke(['rollback',g.deployment,'--to','1','--check']);assert.equal(rollback.code,1);assert.match(rollback.stderr,/minPressure/);
+    await h.host.stop();active.delete(h.host);
+
+    const c=fixture('contracts');
+    // Execute both Quarry blocks from the installed guide, without source substitutions.
+    const guide=(await c.run(['guide','deploy','--section','Keep v1 clients working'])).stdout;
+    const blocks=[...guide.matchAll(/```quarry\n([\s\S]*?)```/g)].map(m=>m[1]);assert.equal(blocks.length,2,guide);
+    c.source(blocks[0]);await c.run(['deploy',c.deployment]);
+    const oldHost=await c.serve();const frozenArgs={name:'Ada',obsolete:'old input'};
+    assert.deepEqual((await oldHost.post('/q/greet',frozenArgs)).data,{message:'Ada!'});
+    c.source(blocks[1]);
+    const safe=JSON.parse((await c.run(['deploy',c.deployment,'--check','--json'])).stdout);assert.deepEqual(safe.clientCompatibility,[]);
+    await c.run(['deploy',c.deployment]);
+    assert.deepEqual((await oldHost.post('/q/greet',frozenArgs)).data,{message:'Ada!'});
+    assert.deepEqual((await oldHost.post('/q/greeting',{name:'Ada'})).data,{message:'Ada!'});
+    await oldHost.host.stop();active.delete(oldHost.host);
+    const unsafe=blocks[1].replace("suffix: text <=1 default '!'","suffix: text <=1").replace(', obsolete: text <=40 deprecated','');
+    c.source(unsafe);
+    const diff=JSON.parse((await c.run(['deploy',c.deployment,'--check','--json'])).stdout).clientCompatibility.join('\n');
+    assert.match(diff,/greet.*argument suffix retyped or narrowed/);assert.match(diff,/greet.*removed argument obsolete/);
+    mkdirSync(join(c.cwd,'versions/v1'),{recursive:true});writeFileSync(join(c.cwd,'versions/v1/schema.q'),blocks[0]);
+    writeFileSync(join(c.cwd,'snapback/client-compatibility.json'),JSON.stringify({baseline:'versions/v1',since_release:2}));
+    const baseline=await c.run(['check']);assert.match(baseline.stderr,/old-client compatibility \(baseline .*new required argument suffix/);
+    const generated=readFileSync(join(c.cwd,'snapback/generated/api.ts'),'utf8');assert.match(generated,/suffix: string/);assert.doesNotMatch(generated,/suffix\?:/);
+    const d=fixture('projected-domains');
+    const rows=`use identity guests
+table items:
+  n: int 0..10
+  label: text 1..20
+  public 'result domain proof'
+query list():
+  return items first 10 by .id {n, label}
+`;
+    d.source(rows);await d.run(['deploy',d.deployment]);
+    d.source(rows.replace('int 0..10','int 0..20').replace('text 1..20','text 0..20'));
+    const domains=JSON.parse((await d.run(['deploy',d.deployment,'--check','--json'])).stdout).clientCompatibility.join('\n');
+    assert.match(domains,/RESULT REVIEW.*items.label/);assert.match(domains,/RESULT REVIEW.*items.n/);
+    return {status:'passed',reactivation:['cold','hot','same-id','dormant-values','unchanged'],refusals:['adoption','post-validation-pointer','no-candidate-left'],rollbackForesight:'dropped-required-column-and-later-writes',oldClients:'installed-guide-signature-diff-baseline-projected-domains'};
+  } finally {
+    try {for(const host of active)await host.stop();} finally {rmSync(root,{recursive:true,force:true});}
+  }
+}
